\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{i}^{2}\left\{\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\epsilon\right)\right\}_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(p^{-1/2+\delta})\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\prec\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore, with probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta})
, the first term of
\sigma_{1}^{2}
in
eq.

S.3
is negligible. As we rescale the variance of equation
eq.

S.3
to order one, this term can be omitted. We will not need to further discuss this term in our proof.
Furthermore, for
\forall\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Omega_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}\left\{\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\right\}_{i}^{4}}{\|\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\|_{2}^{4}}}\right)

(S.4)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)_{j}^{4}+3\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}\right\}+O_{p}\left(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}{n_{z}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}\left(n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}}\right).

Recall that we have shown
\mathbb{P}(\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Omega_{1})\geq 1-O_{p}(n_{z}^{-2\delta})
for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
.
Therefore, consider
\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Omega_{1}
, the second probability in
eq.

S.2
can be further replaced by

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\epsilon)-n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\epsilon)}{\sigma_{1}}\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma}\right.

\displaystyle<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\left.\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\epsilon)-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right),

where

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{2}=

\displaystyle n_{z}\Bigg[\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{i}^{2}\left\{\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\right\}_{i}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\right\}^{2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg].

Using
eq.

S.3
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)-n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)}{\sigma}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

(S.5)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}+O_{p}\left(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}\right)-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(n_{z}^{-1/2},n_{z}^{-2\delta}\right)\right).

S.6.4
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training error

Conditional on
\bm{X}_{0}
and
\bm{\beta}
, we now focus on the concentration over the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
and the corresponding Gaussian generated by this randomness.
By Markov’s inequality, for
\forall\epsilon_{1}>0

and

0<\delta<1/2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\epsilon}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\right|<n^{3/2}m^{\delta}\epsilon_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}}{n^{3}m^{2\delta}\epsilon_{1}^{2}}

\displaystyle=1-O_{p}\left(\frac{m^{1-2\delta}}{p}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(m^{-2\delta}).

Therefore, consider the subset

\displaystyle\Omega_{2}(\epsilon_{1})\coloneqq\left\{\bm{\epsilon}:\bigl|\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}-\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})\bigr|\leq n^{3/2}m^{\delta}\epsilon_{1}\right\}.

Then for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2),\epsilon_{1}>0
, we have
\mathbb{P}(\epsilon\in\Omega_{2})\geq 1-O_{p}(m^{-2\delta})
.
Therefore, for
\forall\bm{\epsilon}\in\Omega_{1}
, we can simplify the Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.4
as follows

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)_{j}^{4}+3\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}\right\}+O_{p}\left(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}{n_{z}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}\left(n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}}\right)

(S.6)

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}}{n_{z}^{2}\left\{\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right\}^{2}+O_{p}\left(n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}+n_{z}^{2}n^{7/2}m^{\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}}\right).

Now with the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
, by applying Lemma S
6
, we have the following Berry-Esseen bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\epsilon}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{3}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\epsilon}(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon^{4}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{4}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}}}\right),

(S.7)

where
\sigma_{3}^{2}=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}.

Next, we analyze the non-asymptotic behavior of
\sigma_{1}
, which is defined in
eq.

S.3
.
Notice that the first term in
\sigma_{1}
is of lower order than the last one. Thus, we will omit it in the analysis from now on. Using Markov’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1}\epsilon_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{n^{3}m^{2+2\delta}p^{-2}\epsilon_{3}^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq 1-O_{p}\left(\frac{(nm/p)^{3}}{n^{3}m^{2+2\delta}p^{-2}}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(m^{-2\delta}).

We further restrict our choice of
\bm{\epsilon}
to a smaller subset where

\Omega_{3}(\epsilon_{3})\coloneqq\left\{\bm{\epsilon}:\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1}\epsilon_{3}\right\}.

For
\forall\bm{\epsilon}\in\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{1}^{2}=

\displaystyle n_{z}\left[2\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}+2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\right)\right\}+O_{p}\left(n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1}\right)\right].

Moreover, we have
\mathbb{P}\left(\epsilon\in\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}\left(m^{-2\delta}\right)
.
For simplicity of the proof in this section, we will denote

\displaystyle\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta},\quad\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}=\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}.

Rearranging the terms in the second probability of
eq.

S.5
and considering
\bm{\epsilon}\in\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3}
, we have the following simplified form

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sigma_{3}}\frac{\sigma_{3}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left[2\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}+2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\right)\right\}+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1})\right]}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right).

By
eq.

S.7
, we have the following inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sigma_{3}}\frac{\sigma_{3}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}-\right.\right.

(S.8)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{n_{z}\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{3}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left[2\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}+2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})\right\}+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1})\right]}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}\right)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{4}}{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}}},m^{-2\delta}\right)\right).

S.6.5
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training data matrix

Conditional on
\bm{\beta}
, we now focus on the concentration over the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
and the corresponding Gaussian generated by its randomness. By Lemma S
2
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{2}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\bm{X}_{0}}(t)\right|\leq O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

where
\sigma_{2}^{2}=n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{i}^{2}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{i}^{2}+2\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}
.
By Lemma S
12
, the first term of
\sigma_{2}^{2}
is negligible.
Moreover, by Lemma S
7
and considering the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
, for
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2}>0
and
0<\delta<1/2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(x_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{4}-n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{j}^{4}+3\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}\right\}\right|\leq\left(\frac{m}{p}\right)^{2}n^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-O_{p}(n^{-2\delta})

\displaystyle\mbox{and}\quad\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|<\frac{m}{p}n^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(n^{-2\delta}).

For
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\cdots,\epsilon_{6}>0
, we further define the subset

\displaystyle\Omega_{4}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\cdots,\epsilon_{6})\coloneqq

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{X}_{0}:\left\{\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(x_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{4}-n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{j}^{4}+3\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}\right\}\right|<\left(\frac{m}{p}\right)^{2}n^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|<\frac{m}{p}n^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon_{2}\right\}\cap\left\{\left|{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\right)-n{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\right)\right|<n^{1/2+\delta}p\epsilon_{3}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cap\left\{\left|\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}\right|<n^{1/2+\delta}\frac{m}{p}\epsilon_{4}\right\}\cap\left\{\left|\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}-n\left(n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)\right|<n^{3/2+\delta}\frac{m}{p}\epsilon_{5}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta}\right|<n^{1/2+\delta}\frac{m}{p}\epsilon_{6}\right\}\right\}.

Note that for
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{0}}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}
, the term with the highest order is

\displaystyle\left\{n\left(n\bm{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)\right\}^{2}=O_{p}\left\{\frac{n^{2}m}{p}\max(n,p)\right\}.

After careful calculation using Markov’s inequality and Lemma S
7
, we have

\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{X}_{0}\in\Omega_{4}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}\left(n^{-2\delta}\right).

Furthermore, for
\bm{X}_{0}\in\Omega_{4}
, now we consider the concentration of our previous Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.8

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right)^{4}}{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}}},m^{-2\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(\epsilon^{4}\right)\left[n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{j}^{4}+3\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}+O_{p}\left((m/p)^{2}n^{1/2+\delta}\right)\right]}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}n^{2}\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}+O_{p}\left((m/p)^{2}n^{3/2+\delta}\right)}},m^{-2\delta}\right)\right).

Moreover, our previous Berry-Esseen upper bound in
eq.

S.6
can also be simplified as

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}}{n_{z}^{2}\left(\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}+O_{p}\left(n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}+n_{z}^{2}n^{7/2}m^{\delta}\kappa_{1}^{2}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\left[\frac{n_{z}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}}{\begin{multlined}n_{z}^{2}\left\{n\left(n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)\right\}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}n^{2}n_{z}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma})^{2}\\
\\
+O_{p}\left((n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}+n_{z}^{2}n^{7/2}m^{\delta})\kappa_{1}^{2}+\left\{n^{3/2+\delta}p^{2}+(m/p)^{2}n^{7/2+\delta}(1+p/n)\right\}n_{z}^{2}\right)\end{multlined}}\right]^{1/2}\right).

Now we rearrange the second probability in
eq.

S.8
to have the last Gaussian generated from the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma_{2}}\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{3}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left(\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left\{2\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}+2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left(\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right).

Using the Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.7
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma_{2}}\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{3}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left(\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\right.

(S.9)

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left\{2\widetilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}+2\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\alpha}_{1}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left(\widetilde{\alpha}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(n^{3/2}m^{1+\delta}p^{-1})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbb{P}\left(n_{z}\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}+n_{z}\frac{n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta}-n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{n\left\{n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})+O_{p}(n^{3/2+\delta}(m/p)+n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\begin{multlined}n_{z}\Big\{2(n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta})^{2}+2n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{n\left\{n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\}\\
\\
+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})+O_{p}\left(n^{3/2}mp^{-1}(m^{\delta}+n^{\delta}\kappa_{1})\right)\end{multlined}\Big\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max(n^{-1/2},n^{-2\delta})\right).

S.6.6
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of genetic effects

In this section, we take the randomness of
\bm{\beta}
into account. We first define the subset where the desired concentration holds.
For
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3}>0
and
0<\delta<1/2
, consider the subset

\displaystyle\Omega_{5}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3})\coloneqq\Bigg\{\bm{\beta}:\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{3}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}\right|<m^{1/2+\delta}p^{-1}\epsilon_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle\cap\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}\right|<m^{1/2+\delta}p^{-1}\epsilon_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\cap\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\right|<m^{1/2+\delta}p^{-1}\epsilon_{3}\right\}\Bigg\}.

For the simplicity of our proof, we introduce some temporary quantities related to
\bm{\beta}
in this section

\displaystyle\widetilde{\theta}_{i}\coloneqq\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{i}\bm{\beta}\quad\text{for }i=1,2,3.

By Lemma S
4
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{5})\geq 1-O_{p}(m^{-2\delta}).

Therefore, for
\forall\bm{\beta}\in\Omega_{5}
, we have

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{j=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta})_{j}^{4}+3\widetilde{\theta}_{1}\right\}+O_{p}\left((m/p)^{2}n^{1/2+\delta}\right)}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}n^{2}\widetilde{\theta}_{3}^{2}+O_{p}\left((m/p)^{2}n^{3/2+\delta}\right)}}\right)=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})

and

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\left[\frac{n_{z}n^{4}\kappa_{1}^{2}}{\begin{multlined}n_{z}^{2}\left\{n\left(n\widetilde{\theta}_{3}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\widetilde{\theta}_{1}\right)\right\}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}n^{2}n_{z}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma})^{2}\\
\\
+O_{p}\left((n^{4}n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}+n_{z}^{2}n^{7/2}m^{\delta})\kappa_{1}^{2}+(n^{3/2+\delta}p^{2}+(m/p)^{2}n^{7/2+\delta}(1+p/n))n_{z}^{2}\right)\end{multlined}}\right]^{1/2}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n_{z}^{-1/2}).

The randomness of
\bm{\beta}
generates another independent Gaussian random variable, and by applying Theorem S
3
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(n_{z}n\frac{\widetilde{\theta}_{2}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\sigma_{4}}\frac{\sigma_{4}}{\sigma}<t-\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{2}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}-\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\theta}_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\right.\right.

(S.10)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{n\left\{n\widetilde{\theta}_{3}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\widetilde{\theta}_{1}\right\}+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})+O_{p}(n^{3/2+\delta}(m/p)+n^{3/2}m^{\delta})\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\begin{multlined}n_{z}\Big[2(n\widetilde{\theta}_{2})^{2}+2n\widetilde{\theta}_{3}+\widetilde{\theta}_{1}\left\{n\left\{n\widetilde{\theta}_{3}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\widetilde{\theta}_{1}\right\}+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2})\right\}\\
\\
+O_{p}\left((n^{3/2}m/p)(m^{\delta}+n^{\delta}\kappa_{1})\right)\end{multlined}\Big]}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right)-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\mathbb{P}\left(n_{z}n\frac{\sigma_{4}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{\beta}}<t-\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}(2\gamma_{2}^{2}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}(n\gamma_{3}+\omega_{2}\gamma_{1}p)+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{2}p\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left[2(n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2})^{2}+2n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\left\{n(n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\omega_{2}\gamma_{1}p)+np\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{2}\right\}\right]}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(m^{-1/5},m^{-2\delta}),

where

\sigma_{4}^{2}=\left(\mathbb{E}(\bm{\beta}^{4})-3\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}}{p^{2}}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)_{i,i}^{2}+\frac{2\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}}{p^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left\{\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)^{2}\right\}.

Moreover, by using the convolution formula for independent Gaussian random variables, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}n\sigma_{4}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{\beta}}+\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}\left(2\gamma_{2}^{2}+\gamma_{1}\gamma_{3}\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}+\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\left(n\gamma_{3}+\omega_{2}\gamma_{1}p\right)+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{2}p\right\}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}+\right.

\displaystyle\left.\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left[2\left(n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{2}\right)^{2}+2n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\left\{n\left(n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\omega_{2}\gamma_{1}p\right)+np\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\omega_{2}\right\}\right]}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}<t\right)=\Phi(t).

Combine the Berry-Esseen inequalities in
eq.

S.2
,
eq.

S.5
,
eq.

S.8
,
eq.

S.9
, and
eq.

S.10
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\bm{H}_{\text{M}}(t)-\Phi(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(m^{-1/5},m^{-2\delta},n_{z}^{-1/2},n_{z}^{-2\delta},n^{-1/2},n^{-2\delta}\right)\right)

(S.11)

for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
. Since
\delta
is arbitrary, the optimal choice of
\delta
will be
1/4
, which gives us following Berry-Esseen inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\bm{H}_{\text{M}}(t)-\Phi(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(m^{-1/5},n_{z}^{-1/2},n^{-1/2}\right)\right).

S.6.7
Limits of the denominator

In this section, we provide the limits of quantities in the denominator, which can be used for constructing the limiting distribution of
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{M}})
with Slutsky’s Theorem.
We have shown that
for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-nn_{z}\left\{n\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+\left(\gamma_{1}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\right)\omega_{2}p\right\}\right|<O_{p}\left(n_{z}n^{2}\left(m^{\delta}n^{-1/2}+\kappa_{1}n_{z}^{\delta-1/2}+\right.\right.\right.

(S.12)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.m/pn^{\delta-1/2}+pn^{\delta-3/2}+m^{1/2+\delta}p^{-1}+m^{1/2+\delta}p^{-1}\right)\right)\right)\geq 1-O_{p}\left(\max\left(m^{-2\delta},n_{z}^{-2\delta},n^{-2\delta},p^{-2\delta}\right)\right).

Similarly, for
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3}>0
, consider the subset

\displaystyle\Omega_{6}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3})\coloneqq\left\{\bm{Z}_{0},\epsilon_{z},\bm{\beta}:\left\{\bigl|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{\beta}-n_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\bigr|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}mp^{-1}\epsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\left\{\bigl|\epsilon_{z}^{\top}\epsilon_{z}-n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\bigr|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon_{2}\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\cap\left\{\bigl|\bm{\beta}^{\top}Z^{\top}\epsilon_{z}\bigr|>n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}\epsilon_{3}\right\}\right\},

then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left\{\bm{Z}_{0},\epsilon_{z},\bm{\beta}\right\}\in\Omega_{6}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(n_{z}^{-2\delta}).

(S.13)

Moreover, for
\forall\left\{\bm{Z}_{0},\epsilon_{z},\bm{\beta}\right\}\in\Omega_{6}
, we have

\left|\|\bm{Z}\bm{\beta}+\epsilon_{z}\|_{2}^{2}-n_{z}\left(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}+\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\right)\right|\leq O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}).

S.6.8
Major quantitative CLT

Combining
eq.

S.11
,
eq.

S.12
and
eq.

S.13
, by Slutsky’s Theorem, we have the following major quantitative CLT for
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{M}})
.

Theorem S4
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
, consider the marginal estimator
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{M}}=n^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}y
, and its corresponding prediction accuracy
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{M}})
. Let

\displaystyle\eta=\frac{(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})/h_{\bm{\beta}_{z}}^{2}\left\{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+(p/n)(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})/h_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\omega_{2}\right\}}{\begin{multlined}2\gamma_{2}^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}(n_{z}/n+1)+(n_{z}/n)\gamma_{3}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}(\gamma_{1}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2})+(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})/h_{\bm{\beta}_{z}}^{2}\left\{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{3}+(p/n)(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})/h_{\beta}^{2}\omega_{2}\right\}\\
\\
\qquad+n_{z}\Big\{\big(\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{\beta}^{4}\right)-3\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}/p^{2}\big)\sum_{i=1}^{m}(\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})_{i,i}^{2}+2\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}/p^{2}{\rm Tr}((\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})^{2})\Big\}\end{multlined}},

As
\min(n,n_{z},p,m)\to\infty
, the following Berry-Esseen inequality holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\sqrt{n_{z}\eta}\left(A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{M})-h_{\bm{\beta}_{z}}\gamma_{2}\left\{\left(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{h_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}\frac{p}{n}\omega_{2}+\gamma_{3}\right)\gamma_{1}\right\}^{-1/2}\right)<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max(n_{z}^{-1/2},n^{-1/2},m^{-1/5})\right).

Theorem S
4
leads to the Theorem
3
in Section
3.3
.
For the special case
\bm{\Sigma}=\mathbb{I}_{p}
, we do not need to use the martingale CLT. Therefore, we can apply the results in Lemma S
11
to obtain Corollary
3.2
.

Appendix S.7
Proof for Section
4.1

For this section, we consider the genetically predicted value
\bm{z}^{\top}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda)
of the reference panel-based estimator given by
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda)=(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n\lambda\mathbb{I}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}
. Similar to Section
S.4
, we prove Theorem
4
in three steps using a leave-one-out strategy.
First, we decompose
\bm{z}^{\top}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda)
into two parts: one related to the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
and one without. Next, we provide a quantitative CLT regarding the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
, considering
\bm{X}_{0}
as fixed. Following the leave-one-out technique, we then consider the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
to retrieve the corresponding CLT results. Finally, we provide the concentration for the randomness of
\bm{W}_{0}
using the anisotropic local law
[
35
]
.
We will frequently use Schur’s complement formula when computing limits using the anisotropic local law.

Proposition S5

(Schur’s complement formula,
[
47
]
)
.

Suppose
p
and
q
are non-negative integers, and suppose
\bm{A},\bm{B},\bm{C},
and
\bm{D}
are
p\times p
,
p\times q
,
q\times p
, and
q\times q
matrices of real numbers, respectively. Moreover, we assume
\bm{D}
is invertible, then we have

\displaystyle\left[\begin{array}[]{ll}{\bm{A}}&{\bm{B}}\\
{\bm{C}}&{\bm{D}}\end{array}\right]^{-1}=\left[\begin{array}[]{ll}{(\bm{M}/\bm{D})^{-1}}&{-(\bm{M}/\bm{D})^{-1}(\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1})}\\
{-\bm{D}^{-1}C(\bm{M}/\bm{D})^{-1}}&{\bm{D}^{-1}+\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{C}(\bm{M}/\bm{D})^{-1}\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1}}\end{array}\right],

where

\bm{M}/\bm{D}=\bm{A}-\bm{B}\bm{D}^{-1}\bm{C}.

S.7.1
Decomposition of new prediction of reference panel-based ridge estimator

Notice that

\displaystyle\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}=\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}+\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon},

(S.1)

By fixing
\bm{X}_{0}
and
\bm{W}_{0}
, we can treat the first quantity in
eq.

S.1
as deterministic. This gives us a good starting point to apply our Lemma S
6
to quantify the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
.

S.7.2
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training error

Considering fixed
\bm{X}_{0}
and
\bm{W}_{0}
, we consider the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
. By fixing
\bm{W}_{0}
, we regard
\bm{R}=(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}

as deterministic. Since
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}
is symmetric,
\bm{R}
is also symmetric.
Considering the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}
, we have the following inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\epsilon}(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mathbb{E}(\epsilon^{4})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})^{4}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{2}}}\right).

(S.2)

For simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle\sigma_{2}^{2}=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z},

and the quantity we are interested in is

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{I}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|.

Notice that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\epsilon}{\sigma_{2}}\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}<\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad t-\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{I}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\right).

Using the Berry-Esseen inequality in
eq.

S.2
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}<t-\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\epsilon}\right.\right.

(S.3)

\displaystyle\left.\left.<t-\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\right)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})^{4}}{\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{4}}}\right).

S.7.3
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training data matrix

Considering fixed
\bm{W}_{0}
, we now consider the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
. By Lemma S
2
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{1}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\bm{X}_{0}}(t)\right|

(S.4)

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(n^{-1/2}),

where

\sigma_{1}^{2}=n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}R\bm{z}\right)_{i}^{2}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\right)_{i}^{2}+2\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)^{2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}.

We now restrict our analysis to a subset where some quantities involving
\bm{X}_{0}
are properly concentrated, allowing us to refine the Berry-Esseen inequality in
eq.

S.3
. By Lemma S
7
, for
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2}>0
and
0<\delta<1/2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}-n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{2}}\epsilon_{1}\right)\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|^{4}}{n^{1+2\delta}/n_{w}^{4}\epsilon_{1}^{2}}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{4}-n\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|^{4}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{4}}\epsilon_{2}\right)\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|^{8}}{n^{1+2\delta}/n_{w}^{8}\epsilon_{2}^{2}}.

Note that

\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{2}=O_{p}(n_{w}^{-4}),

and

\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|^{8}=O_{p}(n_{w}^{-8}).

Therefore, for
\forall\delta\in\left(0,1/2\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}-n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{2}}\epsilon_{1}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(n^{-2\delta})\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})^{4}-n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{x}_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{2}\right\}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{4}}\epsilon_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-O_{p}(n^{-2\delta}).

Denote the subset

\displaystyle\Xi_{1}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\coloneqq\left\{\bm{X}_{0}:\left\{\left|\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{X}_{0}^{\top}\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}-n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{2}}\epsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})^{4}-n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{2}\right\}\right|<\frac{n^{1/2+\delta}}{n_{w}^{4}}\epsilon_{2}\right\}\right\},

and we have shown that

\mathbb{P}(\bm{X}_{0}\in\Xi_{1})\geq 1-O_{p}(n^{-2\delta}).

Furthermore, for
\bm{X}_{0}\in\Xi_{1}
, we can simplify the Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.3
as follows

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}R\bm{z})^{4}}{\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{4}}}\right)

(S.5)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(n^{1/2+\delta}p^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}{n^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}(n^{3/2+\delta}p^{2}n_{w}^{-4})}}\right).

Moreover, we rearrange the second term in
eq.

S.3
to obtain the Gaussian variable generated from randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{1}}\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}<t-\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{nz^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\right)-\right.

(S.6)

\displaystyle\left.\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}+O_{p}(n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-2})}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{n\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(n^{-1/2},n^{-2\delta}\right)\right)

S.7.4
Concentration using the anisotropic local law

In this section, we illustrate how we obtain the CLT conditional on
\bm{z}
and
\bm{\beta}
, considering the randomness of
\bm{W}
. In general, the limit concerning the resolvent can be obtained using the isotropic local law
[
35
]
. Intuitively, as the dimensions increase, the quantity concerning the resolvent becomes increasingly deterministic, leading to an almost sure limit.

Lemma S14
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
, with probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-D})
for some large
D\in\mathbb{R}
, and for all small
\vartheta>0
, we have

\left|-\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+\frac{1}{n_{w}}\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(\frac{p^{\vartheta}}{n_{w}}),

where

\psi(\lambda)=\sqrt{\frac{Im(m_{w}(-\lambda))}{n_{w}\eta}}+\frac{1}{n_{w}\eta}

and
Im(\cdot)
is the imaginary part.

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle\bm{R}_{p}(-\lambda)=\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\frac{\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}\frac{\bm{W}_{0}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}+\lambda\right)^{-1},\quad\bm{G}(-\lambda)=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}-\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}&\frac{\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}\\
\frac{\bm{W}_{0}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}&\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}\end{array}\right]^{-1},

\displaystyle\bm{\Pi}(-\lambda)=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}-\bm{\Sigma}(1+m_{w}(-\lambda)\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}&0\\
0&m_{w}(-\lambda)\end{array}\right],\quad\mbox{and}\quad\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\bm{\Sigma}&0\\
0&\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}\end{array}\right].

By Proposition S
5
, let
B
and
C
be deterministic matrices with sizes of
p\times p
and
n_{w}\times n_{w}
, respectively. Then we can explicitly compute for
\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}
and
\bm{G}(-\lambda)
as follows

\displaystyle\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}&0\\
0&\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}\end{array}\right]\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\bm{G}(-\lambda)=

\displaystyle\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}(-\lambda n_{w})\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}(\lambda n_{w}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}&\bm{B}\\
\bm{C}&\frac{1}{\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}-\frac{1}{n_{w}\lambda^{2}}\bm{W}_{0}(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}+\frac{1}{n_{w}}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0})^{-1}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\end{array}\right].

Denote

\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{z}\\
\hline\cr[2pt/2pt]0\end{array}\right]\text{, }\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\\
\hline\cr 0\end{array}\right],

where
\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}}
and
\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}
are of shape
(n_{w}+p)\times 1
.
From Theorem 3.7 of
[
35
]
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}},\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\left(\bm{G}(-\lambda)-\bm{\Pi}(-\lambda)\right)\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}\right\rangle\right|\prec\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(1),

where
\|\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}}\|\leq 1
and
\|\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}\|\leq 1
. After careful simplification, we have

\displaystyle\left|\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\left\{\left(-\lambda n_{w}\right)\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\left(\lambda n_{w}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}+\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}\right\}\bm{\Sigma}^{3/2}\bm{\beta}\right|

\displaystyle\prec\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(1).

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|-\lambda n_{w}\bm{z}^{\top}\left(n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+\bm{z}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(p^{\vartheta}).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|\bm{z}^{\top}\left(n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\frac{1}{n_{w}\lambda}\bm{z}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(p^{\vartheta-1}).

∎

Lemma S15
.

Adopt Assumption
1
, for
\forall\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
, we have
\gamma_{1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\prec(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\lambda\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\prec\gamma_{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}
for some constants
\gamma_{1}
and
\gamma_{2}
.

Proof.

Since
\bm{\Sigma}
is symmetry, using eigen-decomposition, we have

\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{-1}

and

\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\lambda\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}=\bm{U}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\lambda\Lambda\right)^{-1}\bm{U}^{-1}.

As we have
c\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\prec\bm{\Sigma}\prec C\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}
, it follows that

\frac{1}{\lambda C+1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\prec\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\lambda\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\prec\frac{1}{\lambda c+1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}.

∎

We now provide the first-order limit for the term
\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}
in
eq.

S.6
. We aim to fetch the desired quantity as the form of the derivative of some other quantity, involving only one resolvent
\bm{R}
. After careful calculation, we obtain the following identity

\left.\frac{d(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\bm{R}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}-\tau\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\bm{z})}{d\tau}\right|_{\tau=0}=\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}.

We now focus on
\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\bm{R}^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}-\tau\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\bm{z}
, which can be reorganized in the following form

\displaystyle\bm{z}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1/2}\left\{\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1/2}\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}\bm{W}_{0}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1/2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right\}^{-1}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1/2}\bm{z}.

By Lemma S
14
, and replacing the
\bm{\Sigma}
in
\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}
and
\bm{G}(-\lambda)
with

\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1},

we now set

\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}}=\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{z}\\
\hline\cr[2pt/2pt]0\end{array}\right].

It is easy to check
\|\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}}\|=\|\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}\|\leq O_{p}(1)
.
We denote the Stieltjes transform of asymptotic eigenvalue density of
(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\tau/(n_{w}\lambda)\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}
by
m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)
, which satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)}=\lambda+\frac{\phi_{w}}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\left\{1/\pi_{i}-\tau/(n_{w}\lambda)\right\}^{-1}}{1+m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)\left\{1/\pi_{i}-\tau/(n_{w}\lambda)\right\}^{-1}}.

(S.7)

Let

\bm{\Pi}(-\lambda)=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}-\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1}\left\{1+m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1}\right\}^{-1}&0\\
0&m_{w}(-\lambda)\end{array}\right].

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\vec{\underline{\bm{v}}},\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\left(\bm{G}(-\lambda)-\bm{\Pi}(-\lambda)\right)\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}\vec{\underline{\bm{w}}}\right\rangle\right|\prec\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(1),

where

\bm{G}(-\lambda)=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}-(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})&\frac{\bm{W}_{0}^{\top}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}\\
\frac{\bm{W}_{0}}{\sqrt{n_{w}}}&\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}\end{array}\right]^{-1}\text{ and }\underline{\bm{\Sigma}}=\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}&0\\
0&\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{n_{w}}\end{array}\right].

It follows that

\displaystyle\left|n_{w}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}R^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}-\tau\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1/2}\bm{z}-\frac{1}{\lambda}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}+m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)-\frac{\tau}{n_{w}\lambda}\right)^{-1}\bm{z}\right|

(S.8)

\displaystyle\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(n_{w}^{\vartheta})

with probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-D})
.
For
m_{w}(-\lambda)
, recall that it satisfies

\frac{1}{m_{w}(-\lambda)}=\lambda+\phi_{w}\int\frac{x}{1+m_{w}(-\lambda)x}\pi(dx).

By taking the derivative with respect to
\lambda
, we have

\displaystyle m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)\left\{\frac{1}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}-\frac{\phi_{w}}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\pi_{i}^{2}}{(1+m_{w}(-\lambda)\pi_{i})^{2}}\right\}=1,

(S.9)

where

m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)\coloneqq\frac{dm_{w}(-\lambda)}{d(-\lambda)}.

By taking derivative on both sides of
eq.

S.7
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\phi_{w}}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{-\pi_{i}^{2}n_{w}\lambda}{\left(m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)\pi_{i}n_{w}\lambda+n_{w}\lambda\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\frac{1}{m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)^{2}}-\frac{\phi_{w}}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\pi_{i}^{2}n_{w}^{2}\lambda^{2}}{(m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)\pi_{i}n_{w}\lambda+n_{w}\lambda-\tau\pi_{i})^{2}}\right\}\frac{dm_{w}(\lambda,\tau)}{d\tau}.

Note that our target is
\left.dm_{w}(\lambda,\tau)/d\tau\right|_{\tau=0}
, and notice that
\left.m_{w}(\lambda,\tau)\right|_{\tau=0}=m_{w}(-\lambda)
, we have the following identity

\displaystyle\frac{\phi_{w}}{n_{w}p\lambda}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{-\pi_{i}^{2}}{\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\pi_{i}+1\right)^{2}}=\left\{\frac{1}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}-\frac{\phi_{w}}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\frac{\pi_{i}^{2}}{(m_{w}(-\lambda)\pi_{i}+1)^{2}}\right\}\left.\frac{dm_{w}(\lambda,\tau)}{d\tau}\right|_{\tau=0}.

From our previous observation, we derive

\displaystyle\left.\frac{dm_{w}(\lambda,\tau)}{d\tau}\right|_{\tau=0}=\frac{1}{n_{w}\lambda}\left\{1-\frac{m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}\right\}.

Taking the derivative on both sides of
eq.

S.8
with respect to
\tau
and set
\tau=0
, we have

\displaystyle\left|n_{w}^{2}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}-\frac{n_{w}}{\lambda}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{n_{w}\lambda}-\left.\frac{dm_{w}(\lambda,\tau)}{d\tau}\right|_{\tau=0}\right)\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{-1}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\right)^{-1}\bm{z}\right|

(S.10)

\displaystyle\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(n_{w}^{\vartheta})\implies

\displaystyle\left|n_{w}^{2}\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}-\frac{m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{z}\right|\leq\psi(-\lambda)O_{p}(n_{w}^{\vartheta}).

And on the subset where the
eq.

S.10
holds, we can further simplify
eq.

S.5
as follows

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z})_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{z}\right)^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(n^{1/2+\delta}p^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}{n^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{z}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}(n^{3/2+\delta}p^{2}n_{w}^{-4})}}\right)=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Concentration inequalities in Lemmas S
14
and
eq.

S.10
also lead to that with probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-D})
over randomness of
\bm{W}_{0}
, the following inequality holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{I}_{p}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{W}}}<t\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n\left\{2\theta_{1}+\theta_{0}\theta_{2}\left(m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)/m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}\right)\right\}}}{\lambda n_{w}\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}n\theta_{2}\left(m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)/m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}\right)}}{\lambda n_{w}\sigma_{\text{W}}}\Lambda_{\epsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(p^{-1/2}).

By the convolution formula for independent Gaussian variables, we have the following theorem

Theorem S5
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
4
, recall that
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda)=(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}
, consider the new prediction from the testing data
\bm{z}
. With probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-D})
, we have the following Berry-Esseen bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{z}^{\top}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{I}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\phi_{d}/\lambda\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{w}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{\text{M}}}<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-1/2}),

where

\sigma_{\text{W}}^{2}=\frac{\phi_{d}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\left\{2\theta_{1}+\left(\frac{m_{w}^{\prime}(-\lambda)}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}\right)\frac{\theta_{0}\theta_{2}}{h_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}\right\},

with

\displaystyle\theta_{0}=\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2},\quad\theta_{1}=\left\{\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+{\mathfrak{m}_{w}}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}^{2},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\theta_{2}=\bm{z}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+{\mathfrak{m}_{w}}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{z}.

Appendix S.8
Proof for Section
4.2

To prove the quantitative CLT of
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda))
in Theorem
5
, we use nine steps with a leave-one-out technique. First, we decompose the numerator of
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda))
into two parts and provide the quantitative CLT regarding the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}_{z}
by adopting Berry-Esseen bounds along with first-order concentrations. Similarly, we provide the quantitative CLT regarding the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}_{z}
,
\bm{Z}_{0}
,
\bm{\epsilon}
,
\bm{X}_{0}
, and
\bm{\beta}
, along with the corresponding first-order concentrations.
Next, by using the CLT of the quadratic form, we consider the randomness of
\bm{\beta}
to obtain the desired CLT. We then provide a concentration from the randomness of
\bm{W}_{0}
by adopting the anisotropic local law. Finally, we obtain the first-order limit of the denominator, and the desired CLT of
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda))
follows from Slutsky’s theorem.

S.8.1
Numerator decomposition

The numerator of
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{W}}(\lambda))
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle(\bm{Z}\bm{\beta}

\displaystyle+\epsilon_{z})^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}+\epsilon_{z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{Z}_{0}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z}^{\top}\bm{Z}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}.

Notice that for fixed
\bm{X}_{0}
,
\bm{Z}_{0}

\bm{W}_{0}
,
\bm{\epsilon}
, and
\bm{\beta}
, the first quantity can be treated as a constant, while the second quantity relies on the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}_{z}
, where Lemma S
6
can be applied.
In this section, we denote

\displaystyle\hbar^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}}{\lambda^{2}n_{w}^{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{3}}\left(3\xi_{1}-\xi_{2}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}-2m/p\right)\left\{n(\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})+n_{z}(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2})\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{p}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}(1-2\pi_{1}+\pi_{2})(\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}+\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1})(\gamma_{1}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}})+\frac{2(n+n_{z})}{m_{w}(-\lambda)^{4}}(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p)^{2}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+nn_{z}\left\{\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{\beta}^{4}\right)}{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}-\frac{3\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left\{\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}^{2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right\}_{i,i}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{2\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left((\bm{\Sigma}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+m_{w}(-\lambda)\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})^{2}\right)\right\}\right\}.

The CDF we try to analyze is given by

\displaystyle\bm{H}_{\text{W}}(t)=

(S.1)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{(\bm{Z}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z})^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}<t\right),

We will prove that Kolmogorov distance between this quantity and the CDF of standard Gaussian goes to
0
as
p\to\infty
.

S.8.2
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of testing error

Conditional on
\bm{Z}_{0}
,
\bm{X}_{0}
,
\bm{W}_{0}
,
\bm{\epsilon}
and
\bm{\beta}
, and consider the randomness of
\bm{\epsilon}_{z}
, by Lemma S
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\epsilon}_{z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\|\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\epsilon_{z}}(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}(\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{4}}{\|\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}}}\right).

(S.2)

Now we denote

\sigma_{1}^{2}=\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\|\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}.

By applying
eq.

S.2
to
eq.

S.1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\bm{H}_{\text{W}}(t)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{1}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}<t-\left\{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}y-\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}}{\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2}}\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-\frac{m}{p}\right)\right\}/\hbar\right)\right|

(S.3)

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}(\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{4}}{\|\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}}}\right).

S.8.3
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of testing data matrix

Conditional on
\bm{X}_{0}
,
\bm{W}_{0}
,
\bm{\epsilon}
, and
\bm{\beta}
, we consider the randomness of
\bm{Z}_{0}
. By applying Lemma S
2
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}{\sigma_{2}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\bm{Z}_{0}}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(n_{z}^{-1/2}),

where

\sigma_{2}^{2}=n_{z}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta})_{i}^{2}\left(\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)_{i}^{2}+2\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}.

Consider the subset

\displaystyle\Xi_{2}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2})\coloneqq

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\bm{Z}_{0}:\Bigg\{\bigl|\|\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}-n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}R\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}\bigr|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2}\epsilon_{1}\Bigg\}\cap

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}(z_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{4}-n_{z}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|z_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right|^{4}\Big|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{2}\Bigg\}\Bigg\}.

By regarding the randomness of
\bm{Z}_{0}
, and applying the concentration inequality in Lemma S
7
, for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
, we have the following two inequalities

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\|\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}-n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}\right|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2}\epsilon_{1}\right)\geq 1-\frac{Cn_{z}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right|^{4}}{n_{z}^{1+2\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{1}^{2}}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}(\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{4}-n_{z}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right|^{4}\right|<n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{Cn_{z}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right|^{8}}{n_{z}^{1+2\delta}n^{8}n_{w}^{-8}\epsilon_{2}^{2}}.

Note that

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right|^{4}=\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})_{i}^{4}+3(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}R\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{2}=O_{p}(n^{4}n_{w}^{-4})

and

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\left|z_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}R\bm{X}^{\top}y\right|^{8}=O_{p}(n^{8}n_{w}^{-8}).

It follows that

\mathbb{P}(\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Xi_{2})\geq 1-O_{p}(n_{z}^{-2\delta}).

Intuitively, we restrict our choice of
\bm{Z}_{0}
to a subset where it concentrates well, and we show that this concentration holds with high probability.
Therefore, for
\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Xi_{2}
, our Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.2
can be further simplified

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n_{z}}(\bm{z}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{4}}{\|\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}}}\right)

(S.4)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)\left(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)_{i}^{4}+3\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4})}{n_{z}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4})}}\right).

Moreover, consider
\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Xi_{2}
, the second probability in
eq.

S.3
can be further replaced by

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}{\hbar}<t-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}R\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}\right).

It follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}{\hbar}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\right.

(S.5)

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}\right)-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max(n_{z}^{-1/2},n_{z}^{-2\delta})\right).

S.8.4
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training error

Conditional on
\bm{X}_{0}
,
\bm{W}_{0}
, and
\bm{\beta}
, we now consider the randomness of
\epsilon
. By applying Lemma S
6
, we have the following inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\epsilon}(t)\right|\leq O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{4}}{\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}}}\right).

(S.6)

Now we denote
\sigma_{4}^{2}=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}
for simplicity. We will restrict our choice of
\bm{\epsilon}
to a subset, which is defined as

\displaystyle\Xi_{3}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\left\{\bm{\epsilon}:\left\{\left|\bm{\epsilon}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})\right|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{2}\right\}\cap\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{3}\right\}\right\},

for
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\epsilon_{3}>0
and
0<\delta<1/2
.
By Markov’s inequality and Proposition S
4
, we have

\mathbb{P}(\bm{\epsilon}\in\Xi_{3})\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta})
. It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\left|\bm{\epsilon}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}-\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X})\right|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-p^{1-2\delta}\epsilon_{1}^{-2}\frac{{\rm Tr}\left\{(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2}\right\}}{{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2}}=1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<n^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}}{p^{2\delta-1}\epsilon_{2}^{2}}=1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta}),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{3}\right)\geq 1-\frac{\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}\right|^{2}}{p^{2\delta-1}\epsilon_{3}^{2}}=1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta}).

Rearranging the second term in
eq.

S.5
and applying
eq.

S.6
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}R\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{\epsilon}}{\sigma_{4}}\frac{\sigma_{4}}{\hbar}<t-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{2}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{2}n_{w}^{-2})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sigma_{2}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right.\right.

(S.7)

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p)}{\hbar}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{4}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon}<t-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{2n_{z}(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta})^{2}+n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p)}{\hbar}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{4}}{\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}}},p^{-2\delta}\right)\right).

Moreover, for
\bm{\epsilon}\in\Xi_{3}
, the previous Berry-Esseen bound in
eq.

S.4
can also be simplified as follows

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(z_{0}^{4}-3\right)(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})_{i}^{4}+3(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y})^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4})}{n_{z}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}(n_{z}^{3/2+\delta}n^{4}n_{w}^{-4})}}\right)

(S.8)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}\left\{\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}{n_{z}^{2}\left\{\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta})}}\right).

S.8.5
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training data matrix

Conditional on
\bm{W}_{0}
and
\bm{\beta}
, we now consider the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
. By applying Lemma S
2
, we have following inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}(\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{W}+n_{w}\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}}{\sigma_{3}}<t\right)-\Phi_{\bm{X}_{0}}(t)\right|

(S.9)

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(p^{-1/2}),

where

\sigma_{3}^{2}=n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})_{i}^{2}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta})_{i}^{2}+2(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{2}+\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}.

For
\forall\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\cdots,\epsilon_{6}>0
, we define the subset

\displaystyle\Xi_{4}(\epsilon_{1},\epsilon_{2},\cdots,\epsilon_{6})\coloneqq\left\{\bm{X}_{0}:\left\{\bigl|\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}-n\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\bigr|<n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-2}\epsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\Big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(x_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{4}-n\mathbb{E}\left|x_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|^{4}\Big|<n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{2}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\bigl|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{3}\bigr|\right\}\cap\left\{\bigl|{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})-n{\rm Tr}(\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R})\bigr|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{4}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\Big|\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}-n\left\{n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right\}\Big|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{5}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\bigl|{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})-n{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\bigr|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{6}\right\}\right\}.

By Lemma S
7
and Markov’s inequality, we have the following concentration inequalities

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}-n\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right|<n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-2}\epsilon_{1}\right)\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|^{4}}{n^{1+2\delta}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{1}^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{4}-n\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|^{4}\right|<n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-4}\epsilon_{2}\right)\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}\left|\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right|^{8}}{n^{1+2\delta}n_{w}^{-8}\epsilon_{2}^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\Big|{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})-n{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\Big|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{0}}\left|\bm{x}_{0}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{x}_{0}\right|^{2}}{p^{2\delta-1}\epsilon_{3}^{2}}

\displaystyle=1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta}),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\bigl|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\bigr|<p^{\delta-1/2}\epsilon_{4}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1-\frac{nC\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}_{0}}\left\{(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{x}_{0})^{2}(\bm{x}_{0}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta})^{2}\right\}}{p^{2\delta-1}\epsilon_{4}^{2}}

\displaystyle=1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta}).

For
\forall\bm{X}_{0}\in\Xi_{4}
, the Berry-Esseen bounds in
eq.

S.7
and
eq.

S.8
can be simplified as follows

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{x}_{0_{i}}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{4}}{\|\bm{X}_{0}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}}}\right)=

(S.10)

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\left\{\mathbb{E}\left(x_{0}^{4}-3\right)\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})_{i}^{4}+3(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})^{2}\right\}+O_{p}(n^{1/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-4})}{n^{2}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}+O_{p}(n^{3/2+\delta}n_{w}^{-4})}}\right)\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n_{z}(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2})+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}{n_{z}^{2}(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{4}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})^{2})+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta})}}\right)=

\displaystyle O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\begin{multlined}n_{z}\left\{n^{2}\left(n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\right\}\\
\\
+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})\end{multlined}}{\begin{multlined}n_{z}^{2}\left\{n^{2}\left(n\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}+n^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})^{2}\right\}\\
\\
+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta})\end{multlined}}}\right).

We have shown that
\mathbb{P}(\bm{X}_{0}\in\Xi_{4})\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta})
for
\forall\delta\in\left(0,1/2\right)
. For the simplicity of our proof, we introduce the following temporary notations in this section

\displaystyle\widetilde{\omega}_{1}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta},\quad\widetilde{\omega}_{2}=\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2},\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{\omega}_{3}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}.

Rearranging terms in second probability of
eq.

S.7
and applying
eq.

S.9
, we have the following Berry-Esseen bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}-nn_{z}\widetilde{\omega}_{3}}{\sigma_{3}}\frac{\sigma_{3}}{\hbar}<t-\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\bigl.\left.\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\bigl.\left.\frac{\sqrt{2n_{z}(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta})^{2}+n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{X}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top})\right)+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\bigl.\left.-\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{4}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon}-\frac{nn_{z}\widetilde{\omega}_{3}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}\right)-\bigr.

\displaystyle\bigl.\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{3}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0}}<t-\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}nn_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}R\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-nn_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}/(\lambda n_{w}m_{w}(-\lambda)^{2})\left(m_{w}(-\lambda)\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}-m/p\right)}{\hbar}\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\left.\bigl.-\frac{n_{z}\sqrt{n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\widetilde{\omega}_{2}+O_{p}(p^{\delta-3/2})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon}\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\left.\bigl.-\frac{\sqrt{\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}n_{z}\left\{n\left(n\widetilde{\omega}_{1}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\right)+n\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\right\}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}\right.\bigr.

\displaystyle\bigl.\left.-\frac{\sqrt{2n_{z}n^{2}\widetilde{\omega}_{3}^{2}+n_{z}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\left\{n\left\{n\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2})\left(\widetilde{\omega}_{2}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\right)\right\}\right\}}}{\hbar}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq O_{p}\left(\max(p^{-1/2},p^{-2\delta})\right).

(S.11)

S.8.6
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of genetic effects

In this section, conditional on
\bm{W}_{0}
, we consider the randomness of
\bm{\beta}
using Theorem S
3
.
Before that, we first quantify the first-order concentration of terms that involve in the variance of
section

S.8.5
.
Notice the symmetric quantities related to
\bm{\beta}
concentrate following Lemma S
3
. For
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\right|<O_{p}(p^{\delta-1/2})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{-1+2\delta}),
