\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.\left.+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n\lambda}\left\{{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\right)-\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right\}\right\}\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\bm{\beta}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right)\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{2}\right\}\right\},

and we have shown that
\mathbb{P}\left(\{\bm{X}_{0},\epsilon\}\in\Gamma_{2}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{-1+2\delta})
for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
.

S.10.5
Berry-Esseen bounds with the randomness of training error and training data matrix

Recall that we denote

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}=1-(1-\ell_{1})\phi_{n}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathfrak{h}=\phi_{n}\left\{\ell_{1}-\frac{\lambda\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}}\left(\ell_{1}-\ell_{2}\right)\right\}.

Considering fixed
\bm{\beta}
and replacing
\bm{z}
in Theorem S
1
by
\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}
, as
p\to\infty
, we have the following quantitative CLT.

Lemma S18
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
and S
1
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\mathfrak{u}^{-1}\left\{\left\langle\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{R}}(\lambda)\right\rangle+\frac{\lambda}{\mathfrak{g}}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle-\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|\to 0,

where
\mathfrak{u}^{2}=(\mathfrak{u}_{1}+\mathfrak{u}_{2})/(n\mathfrak{g}^{2})
with

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{1}=\mathfrak{h}\left\{\bm{\beta}^{\top}(\mathbb{I}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}\right\}^{2}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{2}=\left[\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\mathfrak{g}+\lambda\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}\left\{\lambda\bm{\beta}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right\}\right]\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}.

Moreover, following from our concentration results in Section
S.10.4
, and combining
eq.

S.2
,
eq.

S.4
, and
eq.

S.6
, we have the following Berry-Esseen inequality regarding the randomness of
\bm{X}_{0}
and
\bm{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\bm{H}_{\text{R}}(t)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\varkappa_{1}\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}+2\varkappa_{2}^{2}\right)+O_{p}(p^{1-\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}<\right.\right.

(S.6)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad t-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\varkappa_{1}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta}+p^{1-\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{n_{z}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\tau_{0}}{\sigma}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{3n_{z}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta}+p^{1-2\delta})}{n_{z}^{2}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta}+p^{2-2\delta})}},p^{-1+2\delta},p^{-1/2},p^{-2\delta}\right)\right),

where

\displaystyle\varkappa_{1}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-2\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n\lambda}\left\{{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad-\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right\}\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\varkappa_{2}=\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\bm{\beta}^{\top}(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}.

Note that we have dropped the first term in the original
\sigma_{2}^{2}
by Lemma S
12
, where we have shown that we have
(\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta})_{i}^{2}\leq O_{p}(p^{-1/2+\delta})
with high probability for
\delta\in(0,1/2)
.
Rearranging the second probability in
eq.

S.6
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\mathfrak{u}}{\sigma}\frac{\bm{\beta}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{R}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle\right)}{\mathfrak{u}}<t-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\varkappa_{1}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta}+p^{1-\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\varkappa_{1}+2\varkappa_{2}^{2}\right)+O_{p}(p^{1-\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}-\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\frac{n_{z}\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle\right)-\tau_{0}}{\sigma}\right).

Combining Lemma S
18
with
eq.

S.6
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\bm{H}_{\text{R}}(t)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{n_{z}\mathfrak{u}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{X_{0},\epsilon}<t-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\varkappa_{1}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\|\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{\beta}\|_{2}^{2}\varkappa_{1}+2\varkappa_{2}^{2}\right)+O_{p}(p^{1-\delta})}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad\frac{n_{z}\left(\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle\right)-\tau_{0}}{\sigma}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\max\left(o_{p}(1),O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{3n_{z}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta}+p^{1-2\delta})}{n_{z}^{2}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta}+p^{2-2\delta})}},p^{-1+2\delta},p^{-1/2},p^{-2\delta}\right)\right)\right).

Now, the only remaining source of randomness that we have not handled is from
\bm{\beta}
. In the next section, we will present the first-order concentration for quantities related to
\bm{\beta}
, as well as Berry-Esseen bounds for the quadratic form.

S.10.6
Berry-Esseen bounds of quadratic quantities of the genetic effects

By Lemma S
4
, note that
\mathbb{E}(\bm{\beta}^{4})=O_{p}(p^{-2})
, for
\forall\delta\in(0,1/2)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})/p\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1}),\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1}).

Now for
\forall\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2},\cdots,\varepsilon_{5}>0
, we denote the subset

\displaystyle\Gamma_{3}(\varepsilon_{1},\cdots,\varepsilon_{5})\coloneqq

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{\beta}:\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})/p\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{1}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{2}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{3}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{4}\right\}\cap\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{\left|\bm{\beta}^{\top}\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\beta}-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)/p\right|\leq p^{-\delta}\varepsilon_{5}\right\}\right\}

and we have shown that
\mathbb{P}\left(\bm{\beta}\in\Gamma_{3}\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{2\delta-1})
.
For
\bm{\beta}\in\Gamma_{3}
we have
\varkappa_{1}=\Theta_{p}(1)
and
\varkappa_{2}=\Theta_{p}(1)
.
Therefore, we have

\max\left(o_{p}(1),O_{p}\left(\max\left(\sqrt{\frac{3n_{z}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{1/2+\delta}+p^{1-2\delta})}{n_{z}^{2}\varkappa_{1}^{2}+O_{p}(p^{3/2+\delta}+p^{2-2\delta})}},p^{-1+2\delta},p^{-1/2},p^{-2\delta}\right)\right)\right)=o_{p}(1).

Moreover, we provide the concentration of
\varkappa_{1},\varkappa_{2}
and
\mathfrak{u}
for
\bm{\beta}\in\Gamma_{3}
.
Recall the following quantities we have defined in Theorem S
2

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left\{{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-2{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)+\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n\lambda}\left\{{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\right)-\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right\},

\displaystyle\tau_{2}=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left\{{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)\right\},\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\tau_{3}=n_{z}/(n\mathfrak{g}^{2})\Bigg[\mathfrak{h}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{I}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\Bigg\{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\mathfrak{g}+\lambda\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}\Bigg(\frac{\lambda\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma})\Bigg)\Bigg\}\Bigg].

It follows that with probability of at least
1-O_{p}(p^{-2\delta})
over the randomness of
\bm{\beta}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\bm{H}_{\text{R}}(t)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\tau_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0},\epsilon}<t-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\zeta_{1}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\gamma_{1}\zeta_{1}+2\tau_{2}^{2}\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{Z_{0}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad-\frac{n_{z}\left\{\left\{\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle\right\}-\tau_{0}\right\}}{\sigma}\right)\right|\to 0.

Moreover, by Theorem
2
, we have another Berry-Esseen bound for the randomness of
\bm{\beta}

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\begin{multlined}n_{z}\Big[\left[\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\bm{\beta}-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\langle\bm{\beta},\left\{\bm{\beta}-(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\beta}\right\}\right\rangle\right]\\
\\
-\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}/p\left[{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-\lambda/\mathfrak{g}\left\{m-{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\right\}\right]\Big]\end{multlined}}{\sigma}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.<t-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\zeta_{1}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\zeta_{1}+2\tau_{2}^{2}\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\tau_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0},\epsilon}\right)-\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\tau_{4}^{2}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{\beta}}<t-\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}\zeta_{1}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\epsilon_{z}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\left(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}\zeta_{1}+2\tau_{2}^{2}\right)}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{Z}_{0}}-\frac{\sqrt{n_{z}\tau_{3}}}{\sigma}\Lambda_{\bm{X}_{0},\epsilon}\right)\right|\leq O_{p}(m^{-1/5}),

where

\displaystyle\tau_{4}^{2}=\frac{n_{z}\lambda^{2}}{\mathfrak{g}^{2}}\left\{\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{\beta}^{4}\right)-\frac{3\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}}{p^{2}}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathfrak{N}_{i,i}^{2}+\frac{2\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}}{p^{2}}{\rm Tr}(\mathfrak{N}^{2})\right\}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathfrak{N}=\left(\frac{\mathfrak{g}}{\lambda}\bm{\Sigma}-\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\right)\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}.

The quantitative CLT for the numerator follows by applying the convolution formula for these independent Gaussian random variables.

Lemma S19
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
and S
1
, as
p\to\infty
, we have following Berry-Esseen bound

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z})^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-\tau_{0}}{\sigma}<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|\to 0,

where

\displaystyle\tau_{0}=\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left[{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-\frac{\lambda}{\mathfrak{g}}\left\{m-{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\right\}\right]\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sigma^{2}=n_{z}\left(\tau_{1}+2\tau_{2}^{2}+\tau_{3}+\tau_{4}^{2}\right).

S.10.7
Limits of the denominator

We aim to obtain the first-order limits of the following two quantities

1.

(\bm{\epsilon}_{z}^{\top}+\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z})(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z})
and

2.

\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}
.

For the first quantity
(\bm{\epsilon}_{z}^{\top}+\bm{\beta}^{\top}\bm{Z})(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z})
, recall in
eq.

S.13
we have shown that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\|\bm{Z}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z}\|_{2}^{2}-n_{z}(\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}+\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2})\right|\leq O_{p}(n_{z}^{1/2+\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(n_{z}^{-2\delta}).

(S.7)

For the second quantity
\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}
, we have shown that desirable concentration takes place on
\left\{\bm{Z}_{0}\in\Gamma_{1},\{\bm{X}_{0},\epsilon\}\in\Gamma_{3}\right\}
. It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{X}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}-n_{z}\zeta_{1}\right|\leq O_{p}(p^{1-\delta})\right)\geq 1-O_{p}(p^{-1+2\delta}).

(S.8)

Now we are ready to present the quantitative CLT for
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{R}}(\lambda))
.

S.10.8
Major quantitative CLT

Combining Lemma S
19
and concentrations for the denominator in
eq.

S.7
and
eq.

S.8
, we conclude the following Berry-Esseen bound for
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{R}}(\lambda))
.

Theorem S7
.

Under Assumptions
1
-
2
and S
1
,
the following quantitative CLT holds for
A(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{\text{R}}(\lambda))

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left|\mathbb{P}\left(\sqrt{n_{z}\eta_{\text{R}}}\left\{\frac{(\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z})^{\top}\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}}{\|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{\beta}+\bm{\epsilon}_{z}\|_{2}\|\bm{Z}(\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{X}+n\lambda\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p})^{-1}\bm{X}^{\top}\bm{y}\|_{2}}-\frac{\tau_{0}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}}}\right\}<t\right)-\Phi(t)\right|\to 0,

where

\displaystyle\tau_{0}=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left[{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-\frac{\lambda}{\mathfrak{g}}\left\{m-{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)\right\}\right],\quad\eta_{\text{R}}=\frac{\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}}{\tau_{1}+2\tau_{2}^{2}+\tau_{3}+\tau_{4}^{2}},

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left\{{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-2{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)+\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{z}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n\lambda}\left\{{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\right)-\frac{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}}{\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{2}}{\rm Tr}\left(\left(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma}\right)^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\right)\right\},\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}\gamma_{1}+\sigma_{\epsilon_{z}}^{2}.

Recall that we have

\displaystyle\tau_{1}=\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2},

\displaystyle\tau_{2}=\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}\left\{{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-{\rm Tr}\left((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}\right)\right\}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\tau_{3}=n_{z}/(n\mathfrak{g}^{2})\Bigg[\mathfrak{h}\left(\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{I}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})\Bigg\{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\mathfrak{g}+\lambda\mathfrak{m}_{n}^{\prime}\Bigg(\frac{\lambda\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}}{p}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m})-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{n}{\rm Tr}((\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-2}\bm{\Sigma})\Bigg)\Bigg\}\Bigg],

\displaystyle\tau_{4}^{2}=\frac{n_{z}\lambda^{2}}{\mathfrak{g}^{2}}\left\{\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{\beta}^{4}\right)-3\frac{\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}}{p^{2}}\right)\sum_{i=1}^{m}\mathfrak{N}_{i,i}^{2}+2\sigma_{\bm{\beta}}^{4}{\rm Tr}(\mathfrak{N}^{2})\right\},\quad\mbox{and}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{N}=\left(\frac{\mathfrak{g}}{\lambda}\bm{\Sigma}-\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+(\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{p}+\mathfrak{m}_{n}\bm{\Sigma})^{-1}\right)\mathbb{\bm{I}}_{m}.

Here, we present our results in their original form. By simply replacing the quantities defined in Definitions
2.1
,
3.1
, and
S.1
, we derive our results in Theorem S
2
.
